3194 — frankenstein squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3194 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A front-loaded barbell squat held out in front of the body with straight arms. It hits the glutes and quads and demands a tall, braced torso.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the barbell in front of your body with straight arms.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3194.json#/instructions/0

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3194.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3194.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and hold the barbell in front of your body with straight arms. 3194.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keeping your back straight, lower your body by bending at the knees and hips, as if sitting back into a chair. 3194.json#/instructions/1
  3. Continue lowering until your thighs are parallel to the ground, or as low as you can comfortably go. 3194.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment, then push through your heels to return to the starting position. 3194.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3194.json#/instructions/4

3195 — lever lying two-one leg curl

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3195 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A lying machine leg curl that starts with both legs and finishes with one. It targets the hamstrings with a controlled, heavier lowering phase.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the machine to fit your body and sit on it with your back against the backrest. Place your legs on the lever pad, just above your ankles. Grasp the handles on the sides of the machine for support.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3195.json#/instructions/0, 3195.json#/instructions/1, 3195.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Grasp the handles on the sides of the machine for support.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3195.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

Place your legs on the lever pad, just above your ankles.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3195.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3195.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the machine to fit your body and sit on it with your back against the backrest. 3195.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your legs on the lever pad, just above your ankles. 3195.json#/instructions/1
  3. Grasp the handles on the sides of the machine for support. 3195.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keeping your upper body still, exhale and curl your legs up towards your glutes. 3195.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the top, then inhale and slowly lower your legs back to the starting position. 3195.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3195.json#/instructions/5

3200 — lever bent-over row with v-bar

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3200 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A machine row done in a bent-over stance with a v-bar handle. It builds the upper back and lats by pulling the handle toward your abdomen.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a balanced standing blocking pose, preserving any source-documented stance, support foot and torso lean. When standing itself is an authored default, review that reversible choice before motion production.

3201 — quarter sit-up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3201 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A short-range sit-up where you curl only partway up. It keeps tension on the abs and is easier on the lower back than a full sit-up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3201.json#/instructions/0, 3201.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3201.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3201.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3201.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. 3201.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards. 3201.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engaging your abs, slowly lift your upper body off the ground, curling forward until your torso is at a 45-degree angle. 3201.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your upper body back down to the starting position. 3201.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3201.json#/instructions/4
Extracted position and contact cues

Starting Position

  • Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Step 1; 3201.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set up the smith machine with the barbell at shoulder height. 3281.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes slightly turned out. 3281.json#/instructions/1
  3. Step under the bar and position it across your upper back, resting on your traps. 3281.json#/instructions/2
  4. Grip the bar with your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 3281.json#/instructions/3
  5. Unrack the bar and take a step back, maintaining a stable stance. 3281.json#/instructions/4
  6. Keeping your chest up and core engaged, initiate the squat by pushing your hips back and bending your knees. 3281.json#/instructions/5
  7. Lower your body until your thighs are parallel to the ground, or as low as your flexibility allows. 3281.json#/instructions/6
  8. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then drive through your heels to return to the starting position. 3281.json#/instructions/7
  9. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3281.json#/instructions/8

A weighted dip on a straight bar or parallel bars. It builds the chest, triceps and shoulders with added resistance.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Position yourself between parallel bars with your arms fully extended and your body straight. 3313.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lower your body by bending your elbows until your upper arms are parallel to the ground. 3313.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pause for a moment, then push yourself back up to the starting position by straightening your arms. 3313.json#/instructions/2
  4. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3313.json#/instructions/3

3314 — straddle maltese

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3314 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: hanging.

A ring hold with the arms out and the legs straddled. It builds the chest and shoulders and is a stepping stone toward the full maltese.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by hanging from a pair of rings with your arms fully extended and your body in a straight line.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3314.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Start by hanging from a pair of rings with your arms fully extended and your body in a straight line.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3314.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Gymnastic rings with straps. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3314.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by hanging from a pair of rings with your arms fully extended and your body in a straight line. 3314.json#/instructions/0
  2. Spread your legs wide apart, forming a straddle position. 3314.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and slowly lower your body until your arms are parallel to the ground. 3314.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold this position for a few seconds, then push yourself back up to the starting position. 3314.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3314.json#/instructions/4
